# Skills · H · part 8

950 entries, handoff to handoff. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **handoff**: Preserve the context, current state, decisions, evidence, responsibilities, and next actions needed to continue or transfer any kind of work, and resume from such a record. Use when handing work to another person, agent, team, session, or environment, when preparing a continuation record, or when picking up work from one. A request to stop the work for now, in whatever words or language, is not on its own a request for a handoff. ([bartoszarendt/agent-skills](https://github.com/bartoszarendt/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Use when the session ends with work still open or another agent or later session must continue: "handoff", "write it down for the next session", context running out, switching machines. Compacts state, decisions, open questions, changed files, todo status and the next first step into a project memo. Redacts secrets. Not a substitute for finishing work that can be finished now. ([BaSui01/snow-app-skills](https://github.com/BaSui01/snow-app-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Save the session's working state to the project's docs so a fresh session can continue without loss. Use when the user says "handoff", "devret", "save state", "wrap up", "oturumu kapat", "kaydet ve bitir", "context is filling up", or before ending a long session in a project that has docs/STATE.md. Updates only docs, never code. ([Batuhaanccs/claude-context-kit](https://github.com/Batuhaanccs/claude-context-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Prepare a durable, concise transfer to a fresh context with current records, decision authority, evidence, and the next action. ([bbishdotdev/ai-tools](https://github.com/bbishdotdev/ai-tools/tree/HEAD/bstack/shared/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Write a concise handoff summary of the current troubleshooting/debugging activity so the next agent (or a future session) can continue without re-deriving anything. Use whenever the user invokes /handoff, says "summarize where we are", "write this up for the next agent/session", "capture the current state before we stop", or when a long troubleshooting session is winding down with the problem unsolved. ([beekeeper-lab/claude-kit](https://github.com/beekeeper-lab/claude-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/internal/handoff))
